############################
############################
########## MAGRIN ##########
############################
############################

##### Brief History
# Six Magrin tribes migrate to the area
# They split into Mag and Tûmag
# Tumag is ruled by matrilinear queendoms thorough the second age
# In the Numenorean period, as tributaries
# Numenoreans found future capital of Tûmag
# After the downfall, Tûmag is united under the newly proclaimed First Tamska, Pan Veryana

##### TIMELINE (using ingame dates)
# 3893 Birth of Veryana
# 3910 Veryana proclaims herself Tamska after receiving a vision
# 3912 Downfall Start Date
# 3922 Dag Ema Tamska born
# 3938 Veryana becomes first Tamska of Tumag, begins skirmishing in Mag
# 3947 Veryana killed in revolt, Dag Ema becomes Tamska soon after
# 4033 Last Alliance
# 4083 1st Invasion of Pel (Successful)
# 4313 Successful Revolt in Pel
# 4333 2nd Invasion of Pel (Successful)
# 4692 Successful Revolt in Pel, devastates Twin Realms
# 4697 Tamska Jhamena killed, Tamska Ogeda, Goddess of Light ascends
# 4743 3rd Invasion of Pel (Failure)
# 4767 Death of Ogeda, ascension of her Daughter Ogeda II
# 4867 4th Invasion of Pel (Successful)
# 5033 Failed Revolt in Pel
# 5237 Successful Revolt in Pel, invites the Balan-lai
# 5380 Valake Civil War
# 5382 ANGMAR START
# 5630 Tamska Hanan comes to power
# 5674 Trouble in the Twin Nations (Whatever that means???)
# 6095-6102 Lom'Chy (Khyans) migrate over to usakan
# 6730 Lom'Chy migrate across the bay to northern Tumag, adopt Anjiru faith in that area
# 6787 ROHAN START
# 6898 Invading the Islands


###### In order:
# The Tamskas, Rulers of the Twin Realms
#
#
#
#
# Temp Characters
# Dynasty Hukanâwo'os
# Dynasty Osikogasek
# Dynasty Fugowadig
# Dynasty Rufupul
# Dynasty Ongeludiêr
# Dynasty Nurkorul
# Dynasty Tamskakoril, of Ojava and Jebutu
# Dynasty Kûlangier, of Sajokak and Rusajok

# Yeloke Peasant Leaders







######################
#### THE TAMSKAS ####
######################
# Rulers of the Twin Realms
# The Tamskas, or Great Queens, are the spiritual and political leaders of Mag and Tumag. They are chosen as children by a council of elders, though sometimes the inheritor is a daughter

# Line of Veryana
linetamska1 = { # Pan Veryana, the First Tamska - FOUNDS VERYANA FAITH
	name = Veryana
	female = yes

	dynasty = dynasty_tamskal
	religion = faith_tamska_veryana
	culture = tumag

	sexuality = heterosexual

	trait = humble
	trait = ambitious
	trait = zealous

	trait = education_learning_4
	trait = intellect_good_2
	trait = physique_good_2

	trait = tamska
	trait = lifestyle_mystic
	trait = conqueror

	diplomacy = 7
	martial = 5
	stewardship = 6
	intrigue = 7
	learning = 7

	3893.3.23 = {
		birth = yes
		add_character_flag = do_not_generate_starting_family
	}

	3910.6.5 = {
		give_nickname = nick_the_first_tamska
		effect = {
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = had_nickname_event
			}
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_mystic
				value = 65
			}
		}
	}

	3919.5.8 = {
		add_matrilineal_spouse = linetamska1husband
	}

	3947.10.15 = {
		death = { death_reason = death_slaughtered_by_guards }
	}
}
